## Adopted checked rows

Sources: research/20261011-m5-fix-rows.md; research/20261011-m5-settling-pass.md; research/20261011-m5-open-reads.md (CW/FK/TD/PL only). Unadopted last-reads not used.

>  M5-001: clip definition and startup load
>
> ### 1.1 Animation::DefineFromFlatBuf (0x0057571C) and AddKeyFrameFailure
>
> | step | what the original does | citation | record | class |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | name, clear | `this->name = name` (0x0057572E), then `Animation::Clear()` (0x00575734). A redefinition therefore replaces every track. | 0x0057572E; 0x00575734 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | track order | The tracks are filled in the order of the table below. | calls as listed | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | per keyframe | `i` is the 0-based index within that track's FlatBuffer vector. | r7 (r5 for TurnTo; `[sp+0x14]` for BackpackLights) | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | failure | If AddKeyFrameToBack returns nonzero: **sErrorF**, key "Animation.DefineFromFlatBuf.AddKeyFrameFailure" (0x00576290), fmt per track, one arg `%d` = i. Then errG/break, and the function returns that Result. The remaining keyframes and tracks are not read, and the tracks already added stay. | shared errG tail 0x00576174..0x00576180 (BackpackLights 0x00575D10..0x00575D1C); return 0x00576184 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
>
> Track order and the per-track failure format:
>
> | # | track | add call | fmt (address) | sErrorF call |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | 1 | LiftHeight | 0x00575796 | "Adding LiftHeight frame %d failed." (0x005762C0) | 0x00575F2C |
> | 2 | ProceduralFace | 0x005757F2 | "Adding ProceduralFace frame %d failed." (0x005762EC) | 0x00575F72 |
> | 3 | HeadAngle | 0x0057584E | "Adding HeadAngle frame %d failed." (0x00BEE9AE) | 0x00575FBA |
> | 4 | RobotAudio | 0x005758AA | "Adding RobotAudio frame %d failed." (0x00BEE9D0) | 0x00575FFC |
> | 5 | BackpackLights (converted to Json first) | 0x00575CBE | "Adding BackpackLights frame %d failed." (0x00BEEA27) | 0x00575CEA |
> | 6 | FaceAnimation | 0x00575D8E | "Adding FaceAnimation frame %d failed." (0x00BEEA4E) | 0x0057603E |
> | 7 | Event | 0x00575DEA | "Adding Event frame %d failed." (0x00BEEA74) | 0x00576080 |
> | 8 | BodyMotion | 0x00575E46 | "Adding BodyMotion frame %d failed." (0x00BEEA92) | 0x005760C2 |
> | 9 | RecordHeading | 0x00575EA2 | "Adding RecordHeading frame %d failed." (0x00BEEAB5) | 0x00576104 |
> | 10 | TurnToRecordedHeading | 0x00575EFC | "Adding TurnToRecordedHeading frame %d failed." (0x00BEEADB) | 0x00576146 |
>
> ### 1.2 Startup clip load
>
> | step | what the original does | citation | record | class |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | L0 sequence | `RobotDataLoader::LoadNonConfigData` runs: VERIFY not on the main thread, then CollectAnimFiles, LoadAnimationsInternal, LoadAnimationGroups, LoadCubeLightAnimations, LoadBackpackLightAnimations, LoadFaceAnimations, and so on. | VERIFY 0x0051F3AA ("RobotDataLoadingShouldNotBeOnMainThread"); calls 0x0051F3B0, 0x0051F3B6, 0x0051F3BC, 0x0051F3C2, 0x0051F3C8, 0x0051F3CE | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L1 dirs | Two directories, in this order: "assets/animations/" (0x0051F7D8), then "config/engine/animations/" (0x0051F7EC). | 0x0051F4E4..0x0051F4FC; loop 0x0051F57C..0x0051F5A6 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L2 walk | `WalkAnimationDir` takes the resource path (scope 1, 0x00520E66). It calls `FilesInDirectory(path, true, {"json","bin"}, true)`; the extension vector is built at 0x00520EB4..0x00520ECA from 0x0101FF68: "json" 0x00BE7BCC, "bin" 0x00BE7BD1. For each file: `stat`. | 0x00520EFC; stat 0x00520F3A | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L2 stat failure | If `stat` fails: sWarningF, key "RobotDataLoader.WalkAnimationDir" (0x005212A8), fmt "could not get mtime for %s" (0x005212CC), arg the path. The file is skipped. | 0x00520F70 | NEW | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L2 mtime | If the mtime is <= the stored value in map `this+0x70`, the file is skipped. Otherwise the map is updated and the callback runs. At startup the map is empty, so every file passes. | 0x00520F46..0x00520F56 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L2 order | `FilesInDirectory` uses `opendir`/`readdir` (0x00802D70, 0x00802FF6). No sort is called. | 0x00802CF8.. | M5-001 | BLOCKED_EXTERNAL: readdir order is a device filesystem property (already HARDWARE_ONLY in M5-001 unresolved) |
> | L3 collect | The callback does `this+0x44 [type 0].push_back(path)`. Both directories and both extensions go to type 0. | lambda 0x00523F58..0x00523F9A | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L3 info | Then, for each entry of map `this+0x4C`: sChanneledInfoF, channel "Animations" (0x00BE5216), key "RobotDataLoader.CollectAnimFiles.Results" (0x00BE7B49), fmt "Found %zu animation files of type %d" (0x00BE7B72). Args: vector size, then type. | 0x0051F6EA | NEW | RECOVERABLE_GAP: the iteration order of the libc++ unordered_map<int,…> over the 4 types (read the other three lambdas' type values and the insert order) |
> | L4 clip warning off | `ProceduralFace::EnableClippingWarning(false)`. These two calls are the only callers. | 0x0051F8A2; 0x0051F9CE | M5-040 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L5 dispatch | Every type-0 path is pushed in vector order to `DispatchWorker<3>` with job `LoadAnimationFile`. If `IsInSdkMode`, the cache file is added too (SDK: unreachable per the scope rule). Then `this+0x27C = 0.7f / (f32)count` (u32 to f32, then vdiv). | 0x0051F93A..0x0051F946; 0x0051F94C..0x0051F9AC; 0x0051F9B0..0x0051F9C0 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L5 process | `Process()` splits the jobs into 4 contiguous chunks of `n/4`, with the first `n%4` chunks taking one extra. Three go to new threads and the last runs on the caller. It then joins all three. | 0x00521AFC..0x00521BEA | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE for the split. Inter-chunk timing is HARDWARE_ONLY (scheduler). |
> | L6 per file | See the LoadAnimationFile list below. | 0x00521308.. | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L7 registration | See the registration list below. | 0x00588322..0x00588346; 0x00588350..0x0058847A; 0x0058854C.. | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L8 duplicates | `AddAnimation` returns at once if the name exists (0x00587E56..0x00587E5C). The existing object is fetched and redefined: Clear() plus all tracks. **The last definer wins, wholly.** Across chunks the winner depends on thread timing. | 0x00587E4C; 0x00575734 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE for the rule. The order is HARDWARE_ONLY. **Shipped data:** 0 duplicate names among 997 clips (993 .bin, 4 .json; `scan.py` over re-analysis/obb, authority 3), so no shipped clip is affected. |
> | L9 lookup | `GetAnimation(name)` is an exact `unordered_map` find. There is no case folding and no fallback. | 0x0058806E -> 0x00588074 | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | L10 clip warning on, final infos | Steps L10a to L10c below. | as listed | M5-001 | EXACT_SOURCE |
>
> **L6, per file (`LoadAnimationFile`):**
> 1. Return at once if `this+0x278 & 1` (0x00521312..0x0052131A).
> 2. If on the main thread: VERIFY, key "RobotDataLoader.AnimFileOnMainThread" (0x00521818), fmt "VERIFY(%s): " (0x00BE6AD4), arg "!_context->IsMainThread()" (0x00BE7B2F), at 0x00521338.
> 3. Info, channel "Animations", key "RobotDataLoader.LoadAnimationFile.LoadingAnimationsFromBinaryOrJson" (0x00BE7BD5), fmt "Loading animations from %s" (0x00521848), arg the path (0x00521368).
> 4. **If the path has suffix "bin":** ReadFileAsBinary. Then:
>    - data empty: sErrorF "RobotDataLoader.LoadAnimationFile.BinaryDataEmpty" (0x00BE7C19), fmt "Found no data in %s" (0x00BE7C4B), arg the path (0x005213DA), errG/break;
>    - data pointer null: "…BinaryDataNull" (0x00BE7C5F), same fmt (0x00521634), errG;
>    - the root has no clip vector: "…AllClipsNull" (0x00BE7CAA), fmt "Found no animations in %s" (0x00BE7C90) (0x005215D6), errG;
>    - the clip count is 0: "…AnimClipsEmpty" (0x00BE7CD9), same fmt (0x00521722), errG;
>    - otherwise, for each clip, info "RobotDataLoader.LoadAnimationFile.LoadingSpecificAnimFromBinary" (0x00521868), fmt "Loading '%s' from %s", args clip name then path (0x0052153E). Then the mutex at `+0x26C` is locked, `container(+0x58)->DefineFromFlatBuf(clip, clipName)` is called, and the mutex is unlocked (0x00521580..0x00521592). The return value is ignored.
> 5. **Otherwise (JSON):** `readAsJson`. If it succeeds and the value is not empty, the mutex is locked and `DefineFromJson(json, name)` is called (0x0052147A..0x00521490).
> 6. On success, and always on the JSON path, an atomic float add `+0x270 += +0x27C` (0x00521686..0x005216FC). The four binary error paths skip it.
>
> **L7, registration:**
> - **Name.** The name registered is the clip's own FlatBuffer field 0 (0x0052151E..0x00521578), not the file stem.
> - **Reserved name.** `GetAnimationWrapper` first compares the name with `FaceAnimationManager::ProceduralAnimName` (GOT 0x0103EC44; the value was not read). If it is equal: sErrorF "CannedAnimationContainer.DefineFromJson.ReservedName" (0x005884E0), fmt "Skipping animation with reserved name '%s'." (0x00588518), arg the reserved name (0x00588436). Then errG, and the function returns 1 (0x0058834A).
> - **Add.** Otherwise it calls `AddAnimation(name)` and then `GetAnimation`. If that returns null: sErrorF "CannedAnimationContainer.DefineFromJson" (0x00BEF8C8), fmt "Could not GetAnimation named '%s'." (0x005884B4) (0x005883F6), then errG.
> - **Define.** Then `Animation::DefineFromFlatBuf(name, clip)`, and a tail call to `SanityCheck(res, anim, name)` through veneer 0x008CB9AC.
> - **SanityCheck name mismatch.** sErrorF key 0x00BEF8C8, fmt "Animation's internal name ('%s') doesn't match container's name for it ('%s')." (0x00BEF8F0), args the animation's name then the name (0x005885DE). Then errG and return 1.
> - **SanityCheck failed define.** Otherwise, if `res != 0`: sErrorF key 0x00BEF8C8, fmt "Failed to define animation '%s' from Json." (0x005886C0), arg the name (0x00588638). Then errG and return `res`.
> - **JSON clips** (0x005886F4):
>   - the name is the first of `getMemberNames()` (0x00588700);
>   - if the count is not 1: sWarningF "CannedAnimationContainer.DefineFromJson.TooManyAnims" (0x00588878), fmt "Expecting only one animation per json file, found %lu. Will use first: %s" (0x00BEF87E), args the count then the first name (0x0058873C);
>   - debug, channel "Unnamed" (0x005888B0), key "CannedAnimationContainer::DefineFromJson" (0x005888B8), fmt "Loading '%s'" (0x005888E4), arg the name (0x00588788);
>   - with no members: sErrorF "CannedAnimationContainer.DefineFromJson.EmptyFile" (0x00BEF830), fmt "Found no animations in JSON" (0x00BEF862) (0x005887EC), then errG.
>
> **L10, after the load:**
> - **L10a.** `EnableClippingWarning(true)` (0x0051F9CE).
> - **L10b.** Info, channel "Animations", key "RobotDataLoader.LoadAnimationsInternal.LoadTime" (0x0051FBE0), fmt "Time to load animations = %.2f ms" (0x0051FC10). Arg: f64 (t1 − t0), each from `UniversalTime::GetCurrentTimeInMilliseconds()` (0x0051F896; 0x0051F9D2). Logged at 0x0051F9FA.
> - **L10c.** Info, key "RobotDataLoader.LoadAnimations.CannedAnimationsCount" (0x00BE7B97), fmt "Total number of canned animations available = %lu" (0x0051FC3C), arg `GetAnimationNames().size()`. Logged at 0x0051FA52.
>

> - **CWU (M5-040).** A census of BL/BLX/B.W calls to 0x004A88A8 (PLT) and 0x00584B48 finds only 0x0051F8A2 (Enable(0), before loading) and 0x0051F9CE (Enable(1), after DispatchWorker::Process at 0x0051F9C8). The only data word 0x00584B49 is in .dynsym. No runtime toggle; if Process throws, warnings stay off. Also: a parameter missing from the range map skips clipping (0x00584822/0x0058484E). Loading clamps silently; later clamps warn.

ELF GOT relocation 0x0103ED50 binds **0x0105AB20**, agreeing with the settling-pass address; pseudo-0xC106AB20 is not the relocated address. Initial state is warning enabled. |
> | CW2 | `0x00584B48..0x00584B66` | Argument exactly 1 selects 0x00584A41; every other value selects 0x00584BE5 (body 0x00584BE4, BX LR). Assigns the callable through shared GOT 0x0103ED50. No per-face enable flag. |

This is normal-return order, not exception-safe restoration. |
> | CW5 | `0x0058485E..0x005848AC;0x00584A40..0x00584AA0;0x00584ACC;0x00584AEC` | Clip's below-min and above-max branches invoke the same callable with parameter name, original F32 value, minimum and maximum, then replace value with bound. Enabled target widens all three floats toF64 for sWarningF at 0x00584A78: key `ProceduralFace.Clip.OutOfRange`, format `Value of %f out of range [%f,%f] for parameter %s. Clipping.` Disabled target returns without logging. |

The native/decompiler symbol index names the ARM PLT thunk 0x004A88A8 as 0x00584B48's caller, and LoadAnimationsInternal as that thunk's caller; the two actual call sites/arguments are CW3/CW4. ## Call accounting and tests

See calls.md for the complete enclosing-function call census and explicit support/handoff boundary. Regression expectations are from the adopted rows; M5FixRowsTests and M3_013_CD12 verify widths, state/gates and structured log keys/levels/channels/text/arguments. Existing M5AnimationTests cover the unchanged source spans. No new emulator or hardware run. Final test counts/time are recorded in jobs/status/B-M5-1.md.
